+// Divide n elements evenly; the first n%p ranks own one extra element.
+static void block(size_t n, int p, int c, size_t& begin, size_t& count) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(p), r = n % static_cast<size_t>(p);
+    count = q + (static_cast<size_t>(c) < r);
+    begin = static_cast<size_t>(c) * q + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(c), r);

codex
The first build attempt hit an unrelated stale CMake cache in the shared `/tmp` build path. I’m rerunning in a uniquely created build directory, then comparing the complete emitted grid from one rank and multiple ranks rather than relying only on the benchmark’s sanity validator.

codex
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelization in `stencil3d/`.

Key changes:

- 3D Cartesian domain decomposition across MPI ranks.
- Nonblocking six-face halo exchanges using MPI derived datatypes.
- Communication/computation overlap for block interiors.
- Distributed initialization and validation.
- Maximum-rank timing reduction for accurate performance reporting.
- Global grid reconstruction on rank 0 only when `-r` is requested.
- Rank 0 exclusively handles normal output.
- CMake now requires and links MPI while retaining executable name `stencil3d`.

Verified:

- Clean optimized build.
- Validation passes.
- Results match across 1, 2, 4, and 8 MPI ranks, including identical checksums and samples.

+static void block(size_t n, int p, int c, size_t& begin, size_t& count) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(p), r = n % static_cast<size_t>(p);
+    count = q + (static_cast<size_t>(c) < r);
+    begin = static_cast<size_t>(c) * q + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(c), r);

Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelization in `stencil3d/`.

Key changes:

- 3D Cartesian domain decomposition across MPI ranks.
- Nonblocking six-face halo exchanges using MPI derived datatypes.
- Communication/computation overlap for block interiors.
- Distributed initialization and validation.
- Maximum-rank timing reduction for accurate performance reporting.
- Global grid reconstruction on rank 0 only when `-r` is requested.
- Rank 0 exclusively handles normal output.
- CMake now requires and links MPI while retaining executable name `stencil3d`.

Verified:

- Clean optimized build.
- Validation passes.
- Results match across 1, 2, 4, and 8 MPI ranks, including identical checksums and samples.
